\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\frac{2^{r_{1}+r_{2}-1}Reg_{F}}{|\mathrm{disc}(O_{F})|}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{exp}\left(d\cdot h_{Faltings}(E)\right)}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(\Delta_{E/F})|^{1/4}}\cdot\right.H_{NT}(R)^{d}\cdot

\displaystyle\,\,\,\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\frac{B^{1/k}}{(H(f(R))H(f(O)))^{1/k}|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|^{1/d}}\right)^{2d}

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left.+O_{F}\left((H(f(R))H(f(O)))^{(2d-1)/k}\left(\frac{B^{1/k}}{(H(f(R))H(f(O)))^{1/k}|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|^{1/d}}\right)^{2d-1}\right)\right)

The error term simplifies to

\displaystyle O_{F}\left(\frac{B^{(2d-1)/k}}{|\mu_{F}|}\left(\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle R\in E(F)}}{{H(f(R))\ll B^{2}}}}1\right)\left(\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)\subset O_{F}}}{{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|\ll B^{d/k}}}}\frac{1}{N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)^{2-1/d}}\right)\right)

By Theorem
3.30
,

\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle R\in E(F)}}{{H(f(R))\ll B^{2}}}}1=O_{E,f,F}((\log B)^{r/2})

where
r
is the rank of Mordell-Weil group
E(F)
. Also, the number of ideals of norm up to
x
is
\asymp_{F}x
, since the rightmost pole of
\zeta_{F}(s)
is at 1 and is simple. Hence

\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)\subset O_{F}}}{{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|\ll B^{d/k}}}}\frac{1}{N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)^{2-1/d}}=O\left(\sum_{n\ll B^{d/k}}\frac{1}{n^{2-1/d}}\right)=O_{E,f,F}\left(\log B\right).

Thus the error term contributes

O_{E,f,F}\left(\frac{B^{(2d-1)/k}}{|\mu_{F}|}\cdot(\log B)^{r/2}\cdot\log B\right)=O_{f,E,F}\left(B^{(2d-1)/k}(\log B)^{r/2+1}\right).

For the main term, it simplifies to

\displaystyle\text{Main term of }\#\{x\in(\mathrm{Sym}^{2}E)(F):H_{\mathcal{L}}(x)\leq B\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2^{r_{1}+r_{2}-1}Reg_{F}}{|\mu_{F}||\mathrm{disc}(O_{F})|}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{exp}\left(d\cdot h_{Faltings}(E)\right)}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(\Delta_{E/F})|^{1/4}\cdot H(f(O))^{2d/k}}B^{2d/k}

\displaystyle\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle R\in E(F)}}{{H(f(R))\ll B^{2}}}}\left(\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{2/k}}\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\right)

\displaystyle\,\,\,\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)\subset O_{F}}}{{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|\ll B^{d/k}}}}\frac{\mu((a))}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|^{2}}.

Again as the number of ideals of norm up to
x
is
\asymp_{F}x
, we have

\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)\subset O_{F}}}{{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|\ll B^{d/k}}}}\frac{\mu((a))}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|^{2}}=\frac{1}{\zeta_{F}(2)}+O\left(\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)\subset O_{F}}}{{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|\gg B^{d/k}}}}\frac{1}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(a)|^{2}}\right)=\frac{1}{\zeta_{F}(2)}+O_{F}\left(B^{-d/k}\right).

Note also that the series

\displaystyle\sum_{R\in E(F)}\left(\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{2/k}}\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\right)

converges, because

\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{1/k}}\asymp_{E}1

uniformly over
R\in E(F)
(by property of Néron-Tate height), hence

\left(\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{2/k}}\right)^{d}\asymp_{E}\frac{1}{H(f(R))^{d/k}}

decays exponentially in
R
; and

\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\ll_{E,F}1,

by Proposition
B.53
.

Hence the main term simplifies to

\displaystyle\text{Main term of }\#\{x\in(\mathrm{Sym}^{2}E)(F):H_{\mathcal{L}}(x)\leq B\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2^{r_{1}+r_{2}-1}Reg_{F}}{|\mu_{F}||\mathrm{disc}(O_{F})|}\cdot\frac{1}{\zeta_{F}(2)}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{exp}\left(d\cdot h_{Faltings}(E)\right)}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(\Delta_{E/F})|^{1/4}\cdot H(f(O))^{2d/k}}B^{2d/k}

\displaystyle\sum_{R\in E(F)}\left(\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{2/k}}\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\right)

\displaystyle\,\,\,+O_{E,f,F}(B^{d/k}).

Putting both main term and error term together, we get

\displaystyle\#\{x\in(\mathrm{Sym}^{2}E)(F):H_{\mathcal{L}}(x)\leq B\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2^{r_{1}+r_{2}-1}Reg_{F}}{|\mu_{F}||\mathrm{disc}(O_{F})|}\cdot\frac{1}{\zeta_{F}(2)}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{exp}\left(d\cdot h_{Faltings}(E)\right)}{|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(\Delta_{E/F})|^{1/4}\cdot H(f(O))^{2d/k}}B^{2d/k}

\displaystyle\sum_{R\in E(F)}\left(\frac{H_{NT}(R)}{H(f(R))^{2/k}}\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\,\,\,\left(\prod_{v\in M_{F,\infty}}\mathrm{Vol}_{Fal}\left(g_{v}\in H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))\otimes_{O_{F}}F_{v}:\int_{X(\overline{F_{v}})}\log|g_{v}|_{v}d\mu_{v}\leq 0\right)\right)

\displaystyle\,\,\,+O_{E,f,F}(B^{(2d-1)/k}(\log B)^{r/2+1}).

as desired (By Assumption
B.1
,
F
has class number 1; which is why
|Cl(F)|
shows up in the proposition, but not here).
∎

Remark
.

We briefly mention changes needed without Assumption
B.1
.

•

On recasting Proposition
6.4
into a lattice point counting problem.

–

Proposition
B.19
generalizes: for each finite place
v
, consider the irreducible components
C_{v,i}
of
X_{v}
, which corresponds to discrete absolute value
|\cdot|_{C_{v,i}}
on
K(X)
. Instead of
\log|g_{X}|_{X_{v}}
, we consider
\log|g_{X}|_{C_{v,i}}
, weighted by the number of times a section of
f^{*}O(1)
goes through
C_{v,i}
divided by
k
.

–

Corollary
B.23
generalizes: we can require
\sum_{v\in M_{F,finite}}\log|g_{X}|_{X_{v}}
to be equal to a fixed representative of
\dfrac{\text{Vertical divisors of $X$}}{\text{Principal divisors of $F$}}
. To study these representatives, we use the exact sequence

0\to Cl(F)\to\frac{\text{Vertical divisors of $X$}}{\text{Principal divisors of $F$}}\to\frac{\text{Vertical divisors of $X$}}{\text{Vertical fibers of $X$}}\to 0.

This is how class number of
F
shows up in the main term at the end.

–

Corollary
B.26
generalizes: instead of
D_{R}+D_{O}
, we would need to handle
D_{R}+D_{O}+V
for some vertical divisor
V
.

•

On the first successive minimum and covolume of lattice

–

Proposition
B.28
and
B.29
generalizes directly.

–

Proposition
B.34
generalizes. For
\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))
,

*

D_{R}-D_{O}
is no longer perpendicular to all vertical divisors; hence one needs to add an auxiliary vertical divisor to fix this issue, before Faltings-Hriljac theorem can be applied (see
[
52
, Section 7]
). This produces an extra multiple of the shape
\mathrm{exp}(\langle V_{0},V_{0}\rangle)
for some vertical divisor
V_{0}=V_{0}(R)
in the final expression.

*

Szpiro’s theorem (Proposition
B.18
) does not apply when
E
is not semi-stable, and we need to keep the
\langle O_{X}(D_{O}),O_{X}(D_{O})\rangle
without simplifying it further.

We would also need to handle
\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}+V)))
for vertical divisors
V
.

*

The intersection product of
V
with
D_{R}
,
\omega_{X}
and
V
itself can be handled, so the Faltings-Riemann-Roch calculation generalizes.

A particularly nice case is when
V
is a sum of vertical fibers: if
I=\sum_{v}c_{v}v
is an integral ideal of
F
, and
V=\sum_{v}c_{v}V_{v}
is the sum of corresponding vertical fibers, then

\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}+V)))=\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O})))+2\log|N(I)|.

Corollary
B.52
would generalize after the modifications above.

•

The final main term would be different from our simplified case in the following ways: in the sum over
R\in E(F)
,

–

There is an extra multiple of shape
\mathrm{exp}(\langle V_{0},V_{0}\rangle)
, for some vertical divisor
V_{0}
depending on
R
, needed for the application of Faltings-Hriljac theorem.

–

The
|N_{F/\mathbb{Q}}(\Delta_{E/F})|^{-1/4}
term becomes
\mathrm{exp}\left(3\langle O_{X}(D_{O}),O_{X}(D_{O})\rangle\right)
, since Szpiro’s theorem (Proposition
B.18
) does not apply when
E
is not semi-stable.

–

There is an extra infinite sum over
V\in\dfrac{\text{Vertical divisors of $X$}}{\text{Vertical fibers of $X$}}
, where we effectively sum over
\dfrac{\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}))}{\mathrm{covol}_{Fal}(H^{0}(X,O_{X}(D_{R}+D_{O}+V))}
. The sum would be independent over
R
, and can be broken into a product over finite places of bad reduction.
